Cost Formula (V5):
Cost = OutlineArea + α × TotalWireLength + OverflowPenalty + FTPenalty + λ_ft × IllegalFTDeltaArea + γ × EdgeLocationPenalty + δ × Runtime × √OutlineArea

Piecewise OverflowPenalty:
  if overflow_rate ≤ threshold:  β_lin × overflow_rate × OA (linear region)
  if overflow_rate > threshold:   β_lin × threshold × OA + β_tail × √(overflow_rate − threshold) × OA (diminishing tail)

FTPenalty: β_ft × max(0, FT_area_sum − OA)
IllegalFTPenalty: λ_ft × Σ(illegal block delta_area, i.e. MACRO/EDGE used as feedthrough)
EdgeLocationPenalty: γ × Σ(edge violation distances in μm)
RuntimePenalty: δ × runtime_sec × √OA
WireLength: TotalWL = Σpaths nets × HPWL(guiding_points), where HPWL = |Δx| + |Δy|
Guiding Point: midpoint of overlapping segment (shared_edge) or corner intersection (internal_turn), per A41 Figure 6
IncompletionMultiplier (M): Failed cases receive default_cost = worst_base_cost × M

📐 Cost Formula (V5)

Cost = OutlineArea + α × TotalWireLength
     + OverflowPenalty + FTPenalty
     + λ_ft × IllegalFTDeltaArea
     + γ × EdgeLocationPenalty
     + δ × Runtime × √OutlineArea

📐 Cost Formula (V5)

Cost = OutlineArea + α × TotalWireLength
     + OverflowPenalty + FTPenalty
     + λ_ft × IllegalFTDeltaArea
     + γ × EdgeLocationPenalty
     + δ × Runtime × √OutlineArea

📐 Cost Formula (V5)

Cost = OutlineArea + α × TotalWireLength
     + OverflowPenalty + FTPenalty
     + λ_ft × IllegalFTDeltaArea
     + γ × EdgeLocationPenalty
     + δ × Runtime × √OutlineArea
